- Compact & Multi-angle Engraving for Any Project: More flexible to use than competing products, unlocking more usage scenarios; Portable size fits home studios or mobile DIY setups.
- Smart Screen Repeat, 5X More Orders/Day: Save & Reload Designs Instantly for Batch Engraving – Perfect for 50-100pcs Custom Keychains/Wine Tags. Revenue USD15-50/Unit with Zero Setup Time.
- Dual-Laser Engraving + 160x300mm Expanded Workspace: Infrared (metal/plastic) + diode (wood/ceramics) lasers cut 8mm wood; Slide/Rotary Extensions handle 10+ items like keychains & rings simultaneously.
- LightBurn + LDS Smart Ecosystem: 80% Faster Design-to-Profit. Auto-Generate G-code from Your Orders – Use Variable Text & Multi-Layer Editing to Reduce Material Waste by 45%.
- 4-Layer HEPA Air Purifier Eliminates 99.97% Engraving Fumes: Includes desktop purifier with 4-speed adjustment (50dB quiet mode) – removes harmful particles & odors for workshops/store demos.

How customer reviews and ratings work

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

Brian](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AFJVFADJJHBHB34VBC7GZPTGL6VQ?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top)



_5 out of 5 stars_

[**Excellent Build Quality, Great App, and Outstanding Results**](https://www.amazon.com/review/R3QDES6B44J98T/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?ie=UTF8)

Reviewed in the United States on April 23, 2026



[Size: Ultimate Bundle](https://www.amazon.com/product-reviews/B0GHR9274D/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)
[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)








Brief content visible, double tap to read full content.



Full content visible, double tap to read brief content.

















I’ve been extremely impressed with the LaserPecker LP4. The build quality is solid and well-engineered—it feels durable and dependable right out of the box. Setup was straightforward, and everything works as expected.







The LDS app is a standout feature. It’s intuitive, responsive, and makes designing and running projects simple, even if you’re not very experienced with laser engraving software. At the same time, it offers enough flexibility for more detailed work.







The engraving output is consistently sharp and high quality across a variety of materials. I’ve been very happy with the precision and overall finish of my projects.







The Professional Rotary Kit is another highlight. It’s simple to set up and works amazingly well for engraving pens, bottles, and other cylindrical items. It adds a lot of versatility to the machine without adding complexity.







Portability is also a big advantage. The compact design makes it easy to move or store without sacrificing performance.







Overall, the LP4 is a thoughtfully designed machine that delivers excellent results. I would definitely recommend it to anyone looking for a reliable and versatile laser engraver.

## About this Item

- Compact & Multi-angle Engraving for Any Project: More flexible to use than competing products, unlocking more usage scenarios; Portable size fits home studios or mobile DIY setups.
- Smart Screen Repeat, 5X More Orders/Day: Save & Reload Designs Instantly for Batch Engraving – Perfect for 50-100pcs Custom Keychains/Wine Tags. Revenue USD15-50/Unit with Zero Setup Time.
- Dual-Laser Engraving + 160x300mm Expanded Workspace: Infrared (metal/plastic) + diode (wood/ceramics) lasers cut 8mm wood; Slide/Rotary Extensions handle 10+ items like keychains & rings simultaneously.
- LightBurn + LDS Smart Ecosystem: 80% Faster Design-to-Profit. Auto-Generate G-code from Your Orders – Use Variable Text & Multi-Layer Editing to Reduce Material Waste by 45%.
- 4-Layer HEPA Air Purifier Eliminates 99.97% Engraving Fumes: Includes desktop purifier with 4-speed adjustment (50dB quiet mode) – removes harmful particles & odors for workshops/store demos.

## Product Description

output: 0.5mw, class 1
